         import json,glob,os
         tot_c=tot_k=0
         for p in sorted(glob.glob('tests/reports/showcase-interaction-audit/*.json')):
             d=json.load(open(p))
             if 'routes' in d or 'totals' in d:
                 print('SUMMARY', os.path.basename(p), d.get('totals'), d.get('generatedAt'))
                 continue
             c=d.get('controls') or []
             k=d.get('keyboard') or d.get('keys') or []
             ok=sum(1 for x in c if x.get('ok') or x.get('changed'))
             print(os.path.basename(p), 'controls', len(c), 'ok', ok, 'keys', len(k), 'errors', len(d.get('consoleErrors',[])), d.get('generatedAt'))
             tot_c+=len(c); tot_k+=len(k)
         print('TOTAL controls', tot_c, 'keys', tot_k)
         "
